```text
1	"""IMM Training Pipeline for SmolVLA + LIBERO.
2	
3	Usage:
4	    python train.py --config configs/baseline.yaml
5	    python train.py --config configs/imm_anchor.yaml
6	    python train.py --config configs/imm_anchor.yaml --resume checkpoints/imm_anchor/latest
7	
8	Two modes:
9	  - Baseline: L_action only (flow matching MSE), no ACIS/margin loss.
10	  - IMM: L_action + lambda * L_margin with ACIS scoring + token selection + warmup.
11	
12	Depends: lerobot, torch, wandb, peft.
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import argparse
18	import copy
19	import json
20	import logging
21	import os
22	import signal
23	import sys
24	import time
25	from pathlib import Path
26	from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
27	
28	import torch
29	import torch.nn.functional as F
30	import wandb
31	import yaml
32	
33	logging.basicConfig(
34	    level=logging.INFO,
35	    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s",
36	    datefmt="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
37	)
38	log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
39	
40	ARTIFACTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
41	
42	
43	def parse_args():
44	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="IMM training for SmolVLA on LIBERO")
45	    parser.add_argument("--config", type=str, required=True, help="Path to YAML config file")
46	    parser.add_argument("--resume", type=str, default=None, help="Path to checkpoint dir to resume from")
47	    parser.add_argument("--gpu", type=int, default=0, help="GPU index (use CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES for physical mapping)")
48	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42, help="Random seed")
49	    parser.add_argument("--wandb_project", type=str, default="imm-vla-compress", help="W&B project name")
50	    parser.add_argument("--wandb_run", type=str, default=None, help="W&B run name (auto-generated if None)")
51	    return parser.parse_args()
52	
53	
54	def load_config(config_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
55	    """Load YAML config with _base_ inheritance support."""
56	    config_path = Path(config_path)
57	    with open(config_path) as f:
58	        config = yaml.safe_load(f)
59	
60	    if "_base_" in config:
61	        base_path = config_path.parent / config.pop("_base_")
62	        base_config = load_config(str(base_path))
63	        base_config = deep_merge(base_config, config)
64	        return base_config
65	
66	    return config
67	
68	
69	def deep_merge(base: Dict, override: Dict) -> Dict:
70	    """Recursively merge override into base."""
71	    result = copy.deepcopy(base)
72	    for k, v in override.items():
73	        if k in result and isinstance(result[k], dict) and isinstance(v, dict):
74	            result[k] = deep_merge(result[k], v)
75	        else:
76	            result[k] = copy.deepcopy(v)
77	    return result
78	
79	
80	def set_seed(seed: int):
81	    import random
82	    import numpy as np
83	
84	    random.seed(seed)
85	    np.random.seed(seed)
86	    torch.manual_seed(seed)
87	    if torch.cuda.is_available():
88	        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)
89	
90	
91	def _adapt_projections(policy, features: Dict[str, Any]):
92	    """Update SmolVLAConfig features for target dataset dimensions.
93	
94	    SmolVLA pads state/action to max_state_dim=32 / max_action_dim=32,
95	    so projection layers (Linear(32,…)) stay unchanged. We only update
96	    config.input_features and config.output_features so that:
97	      - prepare_images picks up the correct camera keys
98	      - action_feature.shape[0] returns the real action dim for loss trimming
99	      - robot_state_feature.shape reflects the actual state dim
100	    """
101	    from lerobot.configs.types import FeatureType, PolicyFeature
102	
103	    state_dim = features.get("state_dim")
104	    action_dim = features.get("action_dim")
105	    cameras = features.get("cameras")
106	
107	    if state_dim is not None:
108	        old_shape = None
109	        if policy.config.input_features and "observation.state" in policy.config.input_features:
110	            old_shape = policy.config.input_features["observation.state"].shape
111	        policy.config.input_features["observation.state"] = PolicyFeature(
112	            type=FeatureType.STATE, shape=(state_dim,)
113	        )
114	        log.info("Updated state feature shape: %s → (%d,)", old_shape, state_dim)
115	
116	    if action_dim is not None:
117	        old_shape = None
118	        if policy.config.output_features and "action" in policy.config.output_features:
119	            old_shape = policy.config.output_features["action"].shape
120	        policy.config.output_features["action"] = PolicyFeature(
121	            type=FeatureType.ACTION, shape=(action_dim,)
122	        )
123	        log.info("Updated action feature shape: %s → (%d,)", old_shape, action_dim)
124	
125	    if cameras is not None:
126	        old_cam_keys = [
127	            k for k, v in policy.config.input_features.items()
128	            if v.type == FeatureType.VISUAL
129	        ]
130	        for k in old_cam_keys:
131	            del policy.config.input_features[k]
132	        for cam_name in cameras:
133	            policy.config.input_features[cam_name] = PolicyFeature(
134	                type=FeatureType.VISUAL, shape=(3, 256, 256)
135	            )
136	        log.info("Updated cameras: %s → %s", old_cam_keys, cameras)
137	
138	
139	def load_smolvla_policy(config: Dict[str, Any], device: torch.device):
140	    """Load SmolVLAPolicy via LeRobot API, adapt to dataset, and apply LoRA."""
141	    from lerobot.policies.smolvla.configuration_smolvla import SmolVLAConfig
142	    from lerobot.policies.smolvla.modeling_smolvla import SmolVLAPolicy
143	
144	    model_cfg = config["model"]
145	    ds_cfg = config.get("dataset", {})
146	
147	    pretrained_path = model_cfg.get("pretrained")
148	    if pretrained_path:
149	        policy = SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained(pretrained_path)
150	    else:
151	        policy_config = SmolVLAConfig()
152	        policy = SmolVLAPolicy(policy_config)
153	
154	    features = ds_cfg.get("features", {})
155	    if features:
156	        _adapt_projections(policy, features)
157	
158	    lora_cfg = model_cfg.get("lora", {})
159	    if lora_cfg.get("rank", 0) > 0:
160	        from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model
161	
162	        peft_config = LoraConfig(
163	            r=lora_cfg["rank"],
164	            lora_alpha=lora_cfg.get("alpha", lora_cfg["rank"] * 2),
165	            lora_dropout=lora_cfg.get("dropout", 0.05),
166	            target_modules=lora_cfg.get(
167	                "target_modules_regex",
168	                r"(model\.vlm_with_expert\.lm_expert\..*\.(q|v)_proj"
169	                r"|model\.(state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj"
170	                r"|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out))",
171	            ),
172	            bias="none",
173	        )
174	        policy.model = get_peft_model(policy.model, peft_config)
175	        log.info(
176	            "LoRA applied: rank=%d, alpha=%d, trainable params: %s",
177	            lora_cfg["rank"],
178	            peft_config.lora_alpha,
179	            sum(p.numel() for p in policy.model.parameters() if p.requires_grad),
180	        )
181	
182	    policy.to(device)
183	    return policy
184	
185	
186	def load_dataset(config: Dict[str, Any]):
187	    """Load LIBERO dataset via LeRobot API."""
188	    from lerobot.datasets.lerobot_dataset import LeRobotDataset
189	
190	    ds_cfg = config["dataset"]
191	    dataset = LeRobotDataset(
192	        repo_id=ds_cfg["repo_id"],
193	        episodes=ds_cfg.get("episodes", None),
194	    )
195	    return dataset
196	
197	
198	def make_dataloader(dataset, config: Dict[str, Any]):
199	    from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
200	
201	    train_cfg = config["training"]
202	    return DataLoader(
203	        dataset,
204	        batch_size=train_cfg.get("batch_size", 4),
205	        shuffle=True,
206	        num_workers=train_cfg.get("num_workers", 4),
207	        pin_memory=True,
208	        drop_last=True,
209	    )
210	
211	
212	def make_optimizer(model, config: Dict[str, Any]):
213	    train_cfg = config["training"]
214	    params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
215	    return torch.optim.AdamW(
216	        params,
217	        lr=train_cfg.get("lr", 1e-4),
218	        betas=tuple(train_cfg.get("betas", [0.9, 0.95])),
219	        weight_decay=train_cfg.get("weight_decay", 1e-10),
220	    )
221	
222	
223	def make_scheduler(optimizer, config: Dict[str, Any]):
224	    from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import CosineAnnealingLR, LinearLR, SequentialLR
225	
226	    train_cfg = config["training"]
227	    warmup_steps = train_cfg.get("warmup_steps", 1000)
228	    total_steps = train_cfg.get("total_steps", 30000)
229	    decay_lr = train_cfg.get("decay_lr", 2.5e-6)
230	
231	    warmup = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=1e-8, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=warmup_steps)
232	    cosine = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=total_steps - warmup_steps, eta_min=decay_lr)
233	    return SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup, cosine], milestones=[warmup_steps])
234	
235	
236	def wrap_with_imm(policy, config: Dict[str, Any]):
237	    """Wrap SmolVLAPolicy.model with IMMSmolVLAWrapper."""
238	    sys.path.insert(0, str(ARTIFACTS_DIR))
239	    from imm.smolvla_wrapper import IMMSmolVLAWrapper
240	
241	    imm_cfg = config["imm"]
242	    acis_cfg = config.get("acis", {})
243	
244	    imm_kwargs = {
245	        "lambda_margin": imm_cfg.get("lambda_margin", 0.1),
246	        "delta": imm_cfg.get("delta", 1.0),
247	        "warmup_steps": imm_cfg.get("warmup_steps", 1000),
248	        "temperature": imm_cfg.get("temperature", 1.0),
249	        "method": imm_cfg.get("soft_topk_method", "sigmoid"),
250	        "k_ratio": imm_cfg.get("k_ratio", 0.5),
251	    }
252	
253	    wrapper = IMMSmolVLAWrapper(
254	        smolvla_policy=policy,
255	        acis_config=acis_cfg,
256	        imm_config=imm_kwargs,
257	    )
258	    return wrapper
259	
260	
261	def save_checkpoint(
262	    model,
263	    optimizer,
264	    scheduler,
265	    step: int,
266	    config: Dict[str, Any],
267	    info: Optional[Dict] = None,
268	    output_dir: Optional[str] = None,
269	):
270	    """Save checkpoint. Non-fatal on failure."""
271	    if output_dir is None:
272	        output_dir = config["training"].get("output_dir", "checkpoints")
273	    ckpt_dir = Path(output_dir) / f"step_{step:06d}"
274	    try:
275	        ckpt_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
276	
277	        state = {
278	            "step": step,
279	            "model_state_dict": model.state_dict(),
280	            "optimizer_state_dict": optimizer.state_dict(),
281	            "scheduler_state_dict": scheduler.state_dict(),
282	            "config": config,
283	        }
284	        if info:
285	            state["margin_stats"] = {
286	                k: v.cpu() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v
287	                for k, v in info.items()
288	                if k in ("margin", "s_k", "s_k_plus_1", "lambda", "action_loss", "margin_loss")
289	            }
290	
291	        torch.save(state, ckpt_dir / "checkpoint.pt")
292	
293	        latest_link = Path(output_dir) / "latest"
294	        if latest_link.exists() or latest_link.is_symlink():
295	            latest_link.unlink()
296	        latest_link.symlink_to(ckpt_dir.name)
297	
298	        log.info("Checkpoint saved: %s", ckpt_dir)
299	    except Exception as e:
300	        log.warning("Failed to save checkpoint at step %d: %s", step, e)
301	
302	
303	def load_checkpoint(
304	    path: str,
305	    model,
306	    optimizer,
307	    scheduler,
308	    device: torch.device,
309	) -> int:
310	    """Load checkpoint and return the step to resume from."""
311	    ckpt_path = Path(path)
312	    if ckpt_path.is_dir():
313	        ckpt_path = ckpt_path / "checkpoint.pt"
314	
315	    log.info("Resuming from %s", ckpt_path)
316	    state = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)
317	
318	    model.load_state_dict(state["model_state_dict"])
319	    optimizer.load_state_dict(state["optimizer_state_dict"])
320	    scheduler.load_state_dict(state["scheduler_state_dict"])
321	
322	    start_step = state["step"] + 1
323	    log.info("Resumed at step %d", start_step)
324	    return start_step
325	
326	
327	@torch.no_grad()
328	def evaluate(model, dataloader, step: int, config: Dict[str, Any], device: torch.device, imm_enabled: bool):
329	    """Run validation pass and return metrics dict."""
330	    sys.path.insert(0, str(ARTIFACTS_DIR))
331	    from imm.metrics import compute_margin, margin_statistics, topk_preservation_rate, l_eps_delta_ratio
332	
333	    model.eval()
334	    eval_cfg = config.get("evaluation", {})
335	    epsilons = eval_cfg.get("perturbations", [0.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05])
336	    pruning_ratio = eval_cfg.get("pruning_ratio", 0.5)
337	    max_batches = eval_cfg.get("max_eval_batches", 50)
338	
339	    results = {}
340	    total_action_loss = 0.0
341	    n_batches = 0
342	
343	    for batch_idx, batch in enumerate(dataloader):
344	        if batch_idx >= max_batches:
345	            break
346	        batch = {k: v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for k, v in batch.items()}
347	
348	        if imm_enabled:
349	            _, info = model(batch, step=step)
350	            total_action_loss += info["action_loss"].item()
351	
352	            scores = info["importance_scores"]
353	            k = info["k"]
354	
355	            for eps in epsilons:
356	                key = f"eps_{eps}"
357	                if key not in results:
358	                    results[key] = {"margins": [], "preservation": []}
359	
360	                if eps > 0:
361	                    batch_noisy = {k_: v.clone() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for k_, v in batch.items()}
362	                    for img_key in [k_ for k_ in batch_noisy if "image" in k_.lower()]:
363	                        if isinstance(batch_noisy[img_key], torch.Tensor) and batch_noisy[img_key].is_floating_point():
364	                            batch_noisy[img_key] = (batch_noisy[img_key] + eps * torch.randn_like(batch_noisy[img_key])).clamp(0, 1)
365	                    _, info_noisy = model(batch_noisy, step=step)
366	                    scores_noisy = info_noisy["importance_scores"]
367	                else:
368	                    scores_noisy = scores
369	
370	                margin = compute_margin(scores, k)
371	                pres = topk_preservation_rate(scores, scores_noisy, k)
372	                results[key]["margins"].append(margin.cpu())
373	                results[key]["preservation"].append(pres.cpu())
374	        else:
375	            loss, info = model.forward_baseline(batch) if hasattr(model, "forward_baseline") else (model(batch), {})
376	            if isinstance(loss, tuple):
377	                loss = loss[0]
378	            total_action_loss += (info.get("action_loss", loss) if isinstance(info, dict) else loss).item()
379	
380	        n_batches += 1
381	
382	    metrics = {"eval/action_loss": total_action_loss / max(n_batches, 1)}
383	
384	    if imm_enabled:
385	        for eps_key, data in results.items():
386	            margins = torch.cat(data["margins"])
387	            pres = torch.cat(data["preservation"])
388	            metrics[f"eval/{eps_key}/margin_mean"] = margins.mean().item()
389	            metrics[f"eval/{eps_key}/margin_median"] = margins.median().item()
390	            metrics[f"eval/{eps_key}/margin_std"] = margins.std().item()
391	            metrics[f"eval/{eps_key}/preservation_rate"] = pres.mean().item()
392	
393	    model.train()
394	    return metrics
395	
396	
397	class GracefulExit:
398	    """Handle SIGINT/SIGTERM for graceful checkpoint saving."""
399	
400	    def __init__(self):
401	        self.should_exit = False
402	        signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, self._handler)
403	        signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, self._handler)
404	
405	    def _handler(self, signum, frame):
406	        log.info("Received signal %d, will exit after current step", signum)
407	        self.should_exit = True
408	
409	
410	def main():
411	    args = parse_args()
412	    config = load_config(args.config)
413	    set_seed(args.seed)
414	
415	    device = torch.device(f"cuda:{args.gpu}" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
416	    log.info("Device: %s", device)
417	
418	    train_cfg = config["training"]
419	    total_steps = train_cfg.get("total_steps", 30000)
420	    eval_every = train_cfg.get("eval_every", 2000)
421	    save_every = train_cfg.get("save_every", 5000)
422	    log_every = train_cfg.get("log_every", 50)
423	    grad_clip = train_cfg.get("grad_clip", 10.0)
424	
425	    imm_enabled = config.get("imm", {}).get("enabled", False)
426	
427	    output_dir = train_cfg.get("output_dir", f"checkpoints/{'imm' if imm_enabled else 'baseline'}")
428	    config["training"]["output_dir"] = output_dir
429	
430	    # Model
431	    log.info("Loading SmolVLA policy...")
432	    policy = load_smolvla_policy(config, device)
433	
434	    if imm_enabled:
435	        log.info("Wrapping with IMMSmolVLAWrapper (delta=%.2f, lambda=%.2f)",
436	                 config["imm"].get("delta", 1.0), config["imm"].get("lambda_margin", 0.1))
437	        model = wrap_with_imm(policy, config)
438	        model.to(device)
439	    else:
440	        model = policy
441	
442	    # Dataset
443	    log.info("Loading dataset...")
444	    dataset = load_dataset(config)
445	    dataloader = make_dataloader(dataset, config)
446	    dataloader_iter = iter(dataloader)
447	
448	    eval_dataset = load_dataset(config)
449	    eval_dataloader = make_dataloader(eval_dataset, {**config, "training": {**train_cfg, "batch_size": train_cfg.get("eval_batch_size", train_cfg.get("batch_size", 4))}})
450	
451	    # Optimizer / Scheduler
452	    optimizer = make_optimizer(model, config)
453	    scheduler = make_scheduler(optimizer, config)
454	
455	    # Resume
456	    start_step = 0
457	    if args.resume:
458	        start_step = load_checkpoint(args.resume, model, optimizer, scheduler, device)
459	
460	    # W&B
461	    run_name = args.wandb_run or (f"{'imm' if imm_enabled else 'baseline'}_seed{args.seed}")
462	    wandb.init(project=args.wandb_project, name=run_name, config=config, resume="allow")
463	
464	    # Training loop
465	    graceful = GracefulExit()
466	    model.train()
467	    t0 = time.time()
468	    last_info = None
469	
470	    log.info("Starting training from step %d to %d", start_step, total_steps)
471	
472	    for step in range(start_step, total_steps):
473	        try:
474	            batch = next(dataloader_iter)
475	        except StopIteration:
476	            dataloader_iter = iter(dataloader)
477	            batch = next(dataloader_iter)
478	
479	        batch = {k: v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for k, v in batch.items()}
480	
481	        if imm_enabled:
482	            total_loss, info = model(batch, step=step)
483	            last_info = info
484	        else:
485	            if hasattr(model, "forward_baseline"):
486	                total_loss, info = model.forward_baseline(batch)
487	                last_info = info
488	            else:
489	                output = model(batch)
490	                if isinstance(output, dict):
491	                    total_loss = output["loss"]
492	                    info = {"action_loss": total_loss.detach()}
493	                elif isinstance(output, tuple):
494	                    total_loss = output[0]
495	                    info = output[1] if len(output) > 1 else {"action_loss": total_loss.detach()}
496	                else:
497	                    total_loss = output
498	                    info = {"action_loss": total_loss.detach()}
499	                last_info = info
500	
501	        optimizer.zero_grad()
502	        total_loss.backward()
503	        grad_norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(
504	            [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], grad_clip
505	        )
506	        optimizer.step()
507	        scheduler.step()
508	
509	        # Logging
510	        if step % log_every == 0:
511	            log_dict = {
512	                "train/total_loss": total_loss.item(),
513	                "train/grad_norm": grad_norm.item() if isinstance(grad_norm, torch.Tensor) else grad_norm,
514	                "train/lr": optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"],
515	                "train/step": step,
516	            }
517	
518	            if imm_enabled and isinstance(last_info, dict):
519	                log_dict.update({
520	                    "train/action_loss": last_info.get("action_loss", torch.tensor(0.0)).item(),
521	                    "train/margin_loss": last_info.get("margin_loss", torch.tensor(0.0)).item(),
522	                    "train/lambda": last_info.get("lambda", torch.tensor(0.0)).item(),
523	                })
524	                if "margin" in last_info:
525	                    margins = last_info["margin"]
526	                    log_dict.update({
527	                        "margin/mean": margins.mean().item(),
528	                        "margin/median": margins.median().item(),
529	                        "margin/std": margins.std().item(),
530	                        "margin/min": margins.min().item(),
531	                        "margin/max": margins.max().item(),
532	                    })
533	                    log_dict["margin/histogram"] = wandb.Histogram(margins.cpu().numpy())
534	                if "importance_scores" in last_info:
535	                    scores = last_info["importance_scores"]
536	                    k = last_info.get("k", int(scores.shape[1] * 0.5))
537	                    topk_vals, _ = torch.topk(scores, k, dim=-1)
538	                    log_dict["scores/topk_mean"] = topk_vals.mean().item()
539	                if "lipschitz_estimate" in last_info:
540	                    L = last_info["lipschitz_estimate"]
541	                    if "margin" in last_info:
542	                        eps_test = 0.01
543	                        k_val = last_info.get("k", 64)
544	                        ratio = L * eps_test * k_val / margins.clamp(min=1e-8)
545	                        log_dict["gate/L_eps_delta_ratio_mean"] = ratio.mean().item()
546	                        log_dict["gate/lipschitz_estimate"] = L
547	            else:
548	                if isinstance(last_info, dict) and "action_loss" in last_info:
549	                    log_dict["train/action_loss"] = last_info["action_loss"].item() if isinstance(last_info["action_loss"], torch.Tensor) else last_info["action_loss"]
550	
551	            wandb.log(log_dict, step=step)
552	
553	            elapsed = time.time() - t0
554	            steps_done = step - start_step + 1
555	            sps = steps_done / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0
556	            log.info(
557	                "step=%d/%d  loss=%.4f  lr=%.2e  grad=%.2f  sps=%.1f",
558	                step, total_steps, total_loss.item(),
559	                optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"], grad_norm, sps,
560	            )
561	
562	        # Eval
563	        if step > 0 and step % eval_every == 0:
564	            log.info("Running evaluation at step %d...", step)
565	            eval_metrics = evaluate(model, eval_dataloader, step, config, device, imm_enabled)
566	            wandb.log(eval_metrics, step=step)
567	            for k, v in eval_metrics.items():
568	                log.info("  %s: %.4f", k, v)
569	
570	        # Checkpoint
571	        if step > 0 and step % save_every == 0:
572	            save_checkpoint(model, optimizer, scheduler, step, config, last_info, output_dir)
573	
574	        if graceful.should_exit:
575	            log.info("Graceful exit at step %d", step)
576	            save_checkpoint(model, optimizer, scheduler, step, config, last_info, output_dir)
577	            break
578	
579	    # Final checkpoint
580	    if not graceful.should_exit:
581	        save_checkpoint(model, optimizer, scheduler, total_steps - 1, config, last_info, output_dir)
582	
583	    wandb.finish()
584	    log.info("Training complete.")
585	
586	
587	if __name__ == "__main__":
588	    main()
589

policy_config = SmolVLAConfig()\n        policy = SmolVLAPolicy(policy_config)\n\n    features = ds_cfg.get(\"features\", {})\n    if features:\n        _adapt_projections(policy, features)\n\n p.requires_grad),\n        )\n\n    policy.to(device)\n    return policy\n\n\ndef load_dataset(config: Dict[str, Any]):\n    \"\"\"Load LIBERO dataset via LeRobot API.\"\"\"\n    from None,\n):\n    \"\"\"Save checkpoint. Non-fatal on failure.\"\"\"\n    if output_dir is None:\n        output_dir = config[\"training\"].get(\"output_dir\", \"checkpoints\")\n    ckpt_dir = Path(output_dir) / f\"step_{step:06d}\"\n    try:\n        ckpt_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        state = {\n            \"step\": step,\n            \"model_state_dict\": model.state_dict(),\n            \"optimizer_state_dict\": optimizer.state_dict(),\n            \"scheduler_state_dict\": scheduler.state_dict(),\n            \"config\": config,\n        }\n        if info:\n            state[\"margin_stats\"] = {\n                k: v.cpu() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v\n                for k, v in info.items()\n                if k in (\"margin\", \"s_k\", \"s_k_plus_1\", \"lambda\", \"action_loss\", \"margin_loss\")\n            }\n\n        torch.save(state, ckpt_dir / \"checkpoint.pt\")\n\n        latest_link = Path(output_dir) / \"latest\"\n        if latest_link.exists() or latest_link.is_symlink():\n            latest_link.unlink()\n        latest_link.symlink_to(ckpt_dir.name)\n\n        log.info(\"Checkpoint saved: %s\", ckpt_dir)\n    except torch.device,\n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Load checkpoint and return the step to resume from.\"\"\"\n    ckpt_path = Path(path)\n    if ckpt_path.is_dir():\n        ckpt_path = ckpt_path / \"checkpoint.pt\"\n\n    log.info(\"Resuming from %s\", ckpt_path)\n    state = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)\n\n    model.load_state_dict(state[\"model_state_dict\"])\n    optimizer.load_state_dict(state[\"optimizer_state_dict\"])\n    scheduler.load_state_dict(state[\"scheduler_state_dict\"])\n\n    start_step = torch.device, imm_enabled: bool):\n    \"\"\"Run validation pass and return metrics dict.\"\"\"\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(ARTIFACTS_DIR))\n    from imm.metrics import compute_margin, margin_statistics, topk_preservation_rate, l_eps_delta_ratio\n\n    model.eval()\n    eval_cfg = config.get(\"evaluation\", {})\n    epsilons = eval_cfg.get(\"perturbations\", [0.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05])\n    pruning_ratio = eval_cfg.get(\"pruning_ratio\", 0.5)\n    max_batches = eval_cfg.get(\"max_eval_batches\", 50)\n\n    results = {}\n    total_action_loss = 0.0\n    n_batches = 0\n\n    for batch_idx, batch in enumerate(dataloader):\n        if batch_idx >= max_batches:\n            break\n        batch = {k: v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for k, v in batch.items()}\n\n        if imm_enabled:\n            _, info = model(batch, step=step)\n            total_action_loss += info[\"action_loss\"].item()\n\n            scores = info[\"importance_scores\"]\n            k = info[\"k\"]\n\n            for eps in epsilons:\n                key = f\"eps_{eps}\"\n                if key not in results:\n                    results[key] = {\"margins\": [], \"preservation\": []}\n\n                if eps > 0:\n                    batch_noisy = {k_: v.clone() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for k_, v in batch.items()}\n                    for img_key in [k_ for k_ in batch_noisy if \"image\" in k_.lower()]:\n                        if isinstance(batch_noisy[img_key], torch.Tensor) and batch_noisy[img_key].is_floating_point():\n                            batch_noisy[img_key] = (batch_noisy[img_key] + eps * torch.randn_like(batch_noisy[img_key])).clamp(0, 1)\n                    _, info_noisy = model(batch_noisy, step=step)\n                    scores_noisy = info_noisy[\"importance_scores\"]\n                else:\n                    scores_noisy = scores\n\n                margin = compute_margin(scores, k)\n                pres = topk_preservation_rate(scores, scores_noisy, k)\n                results[key][\"margins\"].append(margin.cpu())\n                results[key][\"preservation\"].append(pres.cpu())\n        else:\n            loss, info = model.forward_baseline(batch) if hasattr(model, \"forward_baseline\") else (model(batch), {})\n            if isinstance(loss, tuple):\n                loss = loss[0]\n            total_action_loss += (info.get(\"action_loss\", loss) if isinstance(info, dict) else loss).item()\n\n        n_batches += 1\n\n    metrics = {\"eval/action_loss\": total_action_loss / max(n_batches, 1)}\n\n    if imm_enabled:\n        for eps_key, data in results.items():\n            margins = torch.cat(data[\"margins\"])\n            pres = torch.cat(data[\"preservation\"])\n            metrics[f\"eval/{eps_key}/margin_mean\"] = margins.mean().item()\n            metrics[f\"eval/{eps_key}/margin_median\"] = margins.median().item()\n            metrics[f\"eval/{eps_key}/margin_std\"] = margins.std().item()\n            metrics[f\"eval/{eps_key}/preservation_rate\"] = pres.mean().item()\n\n    model.train()\n    return metrics\n\n\nclass GracefulExit:\n    \"\"\"Handle SIGINT/SIGTERM for graceful checkpoint saving.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.should_exit = False\n        signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, self._handler)\n        signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, self._handler)\n\n    def _handler(self, signum, frame):\n        log.info(\"Received signal %d, will exit after torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n    log.info(\"Device: %s\", device)\n\n    train_cfg = config[\"training\"]\n    total_steps = train_cfg.get(\"total_steps\", 30000)\n    eval_every = train_cfg.get(\"eval_every\", 2000)\n    save_every = train_cfg.get(\"save_every\", 5000)\n    log_every = train_cfg.get(\"log_every\", 50)\n    grad_clip = train_cfg.get(\"grad_clip\", 10.0)\n\n    imm_enabled = config.get(\"imm\", {}).get(\"enabled\", False)\n\n    output_dir = train_cfg.get(\"output_dir\", f\"checkpoints/{'imm' if imm_enabled else 'baseline'}\")\n    config[\"training\"][\"output_dir\"] = output_dir\n\n    # Model\n    log.info(\"Loading SmolVLA policy...\")\n    policy = load_smolvla_policy(config, device)\n\n    if imm_enabled:\n        log.info(\"Wrapping with IMMSmolVLAWrapper (delta=%.2f, lambda=%.2f)\",\n                 config[\"imm\"].get(\"delta\", 1.0), config[\"imm\"].get(\"lambda_margin\", 0.1))\n        model = wrap_with_imm(policy, config)\n        model.to(device)\n    else:\n        model = policy\n\n    # Dataset\n    log.info(\"Loading dataset...\")\n    dataset = load_dataset(config)\n    dataloader = make_dataloader(dataset, config)\n    dataloader_iter = iter(dataloader)\n\n    eval_dataset = load_dataset(config)\n    eval_dataloader = make_dataloader(eval_dataset, {**config, \"training\": {**train_cfg, \"batch_size\": train_cfg.get(\"eval_batch_size\", train_cfg.get(\"batch_size\", 4))}})\n\n    # Optimizer / Scheduler\n    optimizer = make_optimizer(model, config)\n    scheduler = make_scheduler(optimizer, config)\n\n    # Resume\n    start_step = 0\n    if args.resume:\n        start_step = load_checkpoint(args.resume, model, optimizer, scheduler, device)\n\n    # W&B\n    run_name = args.wandb_run or (f\"{'imm' if imm_enabled else 'baseline'}_seed{args.seed}\")\n    wandb.init(project=args.wandb_project, dataloader_iter = iter(dataloader)\n            batch = next(dataloader_iter)\n\n        batch = {k: v.to(device) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v for k, v in batch.items()}\n\n        if imm_enabled:\n            total_loss, info = model(batch, step=step)\n            last_info = info\n        else:\n            if hasattr(model, \"forward_baseline\"):\n                total_loss, info = model.forward_baseline(batch)\n                last_info = info\n            else:\n                output = model(batch)\n                if isinstance(output, dict):\n                    total_loss = output[\"loss\"]\n                    info = {\"action_loss\": total_loss.detach()}\n                elif isinstance(output, tuple):\n                    total_loss = output[0]\n                    info = output[1] if len(output) > 1 else {\"action_loss\": total_loss.detach()}\n                else:\n                    total_loss = output\n                    info = {\"action_loss\": total_loss.detach()}\n                last_info = info\n\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\n        total_loss.backward()\n        grad_norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(\n            [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], grad_clip\n        )\n        optimizer.step()\n        scheduler.step()\n\n        # Logging\n        if step % log_every == 0:\n            log_dict = {\n optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"],\n                \"train/step\": step,\n            }\n\n            if imm_enabled and isinstance(last_info, dict):\n                log_dict.update({\n           \"train/lambda\": last_info.get(\"lambda\", torch.tensor(0.0)).item(),\n                })\n                if \"margin\" in last_info:\n                    margins = last_info[\"margin\"]\n                    log_dict.update({\n                        \"margin/mean\": margins.mean().item(),\n                        \"margin/median\": margins.median().item(),\n                        \"margin/std\": margins.std().item(),\n                        \"margin/min\": margins.min().item(),\n                        \"margin/max\": margins.max().item(),\n                    })\n                    log_dict[\"margin/histogram\"] = wandb.Histogram(margins.cpu().numpy())\n                if \"importance_scores\" in last_info:\n                    scores = last_info[\"importance_scores\"]\n                    k = last_info.get(\"k\", int(scores.shape[1] * 0.5))\n                    topk_vals, _ = torch.topk(scores, k, dim=-1)\n                    log_dict[\"scores/topk_mean\"] = topk_vals.mean().item()\n                if \"lipschitz_estimate\" in last_info:\n                    L = last_info[\"lipschitz_estimate\"]\n                    if \"margin\" in last_info:\n                        eps_test = 0.01\n                        k_val = last_info.get(\"k\", 64)\n                        ratio = L * eps_test * k_val / margins.clamp(min=1e-8)\n                        log_dict[\"gate/L_eps_delta_ratio_mean\"] = ratio.mean().item()\n                        log_dict[\"gate/lipschitz_estimate\"] = L\n            else:\n                if isinstance(last_info, dict) last_info[\"action_loss\"]\n\n            wandb.log(log_dict, step=step)\n\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            steps_done = step - start_step + 1\n            sps = steps_done / optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"], grad_norm, sps,\n            )\n\n        # Eval\n        if step > 0 and step % eval_every == 0:\n            log.info(\"Running evaluation at step %d...\", step)\n 1	"""IMMSmolVLAWrapper — wraps SmolVLA forward pass with ACIS scoring + token selection.
2	
3	Data flow:
4	  Original:  images → SigLIP → connector → embed_prefix → VLM+Expert → loss
5	  Modified:  images → SigLIP → connector → [ACIS → soft top-k → mask] → rebuild_prefix → VLM+Expert → loss + L_margin
6	
7	Takes a full SmolVLAPolicy (not just model) to use its batch preprocessing.
8	Replicates embed_prefix internally to inject masked visual tokens before the
9	VLM+Expert forward pass, without modifying any lerobot source code.
10	
11	Depends: acis.ACIS, losses.IMMTotalLoss, token_selection.MarginAwareTokenSelector.
12	"""
13	
14	from __future__ import annotations
15	
16	import math
17	
18	import torch
19	import torch.nn as nn
20	import torch.nn.functional as F
21	from torch import Tensor
22	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
23	
24	from .acis import ACIS
25	from .losses import IMMTotalLoss
26	from .token_selection import MarginAwareTokenSelector
27	
28	try:
29	    from lerobot.utils.constants import (
30	        ACTION,
31	        OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK,
32	        OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS,
33	        OBS_STATE,
34	    )
35	except ImportError:
36	    ACTION = "action"
37	    OBS_STATE = "observation.state"
38	    OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS = "observation.language.tokens"
39	    OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK = "observation.language.attention_mask"

41	
42	def _pad_tensor(tensor: Tensor, max_len: int, pad_value: float = 0) -> Tensor:
43	    """Pad tensor along dim=1 to max_len."""
44	    b, d = tensor.shape[:2]
45	    if d >= max_len:
46	        return tensor
47	    padded = torch.full(
48	        (b, max_len, *tensor.shape[2:]), pad_value, dtype=tensor.dtype, device=tensor.device
49	    )
50	    padded[:, :d] = tensor
51	    return padded
52	
53	
54	def _make_att_2d_masks(pad_masks: Tensor, att_masks: Tensor) -> Tensor:
55	    """Build 2D causal/prefix attention mask from 1D pad + attention-region masks.
56	
57	    att_masks uses cumsum convention: 0 = bidirectional block, 1 = new causal block.
58	    """
59	    cumsum = torch.cumsum(att_masks, dim=1)
60	    att_2d = cumsum[:, None, :] <= cumsum[:, :, None]
61	    pad_2d = pad_masks[:, None, :] * pad_masks[:, :, None]
62	    return att_2d & pad_2d
63	
64	
65	class IMMSmolVLAWrapper(nn.Module):
66	    """Wraps SmolVLAPolicy with ACIS importance scoring and margin-based token selection.
67	
68	    Args:
69	        smolvla_policy: SmolVLAPolicy instance (may have LoRA applied via PeftModel).
70	        acis_config: kwargs for ACIS (visual_dim, action_dim, num_heads, dropout).
71	        imm_config: kwargs for IMMTotalLoss + k_ratio.
72	    """
73	
74	    def __init__(
75	        self,
76	        smolvla_policy: Any,
77	        acis_config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
78	        imm_config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
79	    ):
80	        super().__init__()
81	        self.policy = smolvla_policy
82	        self.base_model = self._unwrap_to_vla(smolvla_policy.model)
83	
84	        acis_config = dict(acis_config or {})
85	        imm_config = dict(imm_config or {})
86	
87	        if "visual_dim" not in acis_config or "action_dim" not in acis_config:
88	            vlm_hidden = self.base_model.vlm_with_expert.config.text_config.hidden_size
89	            expert_hidden = self.base_model.vlm_with_expert.expert_hidden_size
90	            acis_config.setdefault("visual_dim", vlm_hidden)
91	            acis_config.setdefault("action_dim", expert_hidden)
92	
93	        self.acis = ACIS(**acis_config)
94	
95	        self.k_ratio = imm_config.pop("k_ratio", 0.5)
96	        selector_temp = imm_config.pop("selector_temperature", 1.0)
97	
98	        self.imm_loss = IMMTotalLoss(**imm_config)
99	        self.token_selector = MarginAwareTokenSelector(temperature=selector_temp)
100	
101	    @staticmethod
102	    def _unwrap_to_vla(model: nn.Module) -> nn.Module:
103	        """Unwrap PeftModel → LoraModel → VLAFlowMatching."""
104	        try:
105	            from peft import PeftModel
106	            if isinstance(model, PeftModel):
107	                return model.base_model.model
108	        except ImportError:
109	            pass
110	        return model
111	
112	    def _get_visual_tokens(self, images: List[Tensor]) -> Tuple[Tensor, List[int]]:
113	        """Encode each camera image through SigLIP+connector.
114	
115	        Returns:
116	            vis_tokens: (B, sum(n_i), D) concatenated visual tokens from all cameras.
117	            tokens_per_camera: list of token counts per camera.
118	        """
119	        all_vis = []
120	        tokens_per_camera = []
121	        for img in images:
122	            vis = self.base_model.vlm_with_expert.embed_image(img)
123	            all_vis.append(vis)
124	            tokens_per_camera.append(vis.shape[1])
125	        return torch.cat(all_vis, dim=1), tokens_per_camera
126	
127	    def _build_prefix_with_vis_tokens(
128	        self,
129	        vis_tokens: Tensor,
130	        tokens_per_camera: List[int],
131	        img_masks: List[Tensor],
132	        lang_tokens: Tensor,
133	        lang_masks: Tensor,
134	        state: Tensor,
135	    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Tensor]:
136	        """Reconstruct prefix embeddings using pre-computed (possibly masked) visual tokens.
137	
138	        Replicates VLAFlowMatching.embed_prefix() but substitutes the visual token
139	        computation with externally provided tokens (after ACIS masking).
140	
141	        Returns:
142	            embs:      (B, prefix_length, D)
143	            pad_masks: (B, prefix_length) bool
144	            att_masks: (B, prefix_length) bool/float
145	        """
146	        bm = self.base_model
147	        device = vis_tokens.device
148	        bsize = vis_tokens.shape[0]
149	
150	        embs: list[Tensor] = []
151	        pad_masks: list[Tensor] = []
152	        att_masks_list: list[int] = []
153	
154	        offset = 0
155	        for cam_idx, (n_tokens, img_mask) in enumerate(zip(tokens_per_camera, img_masks)):
156	            if bm.add_image_special_tokens:
157	                start_tok = (
158	                    bm.vlm_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(
159	                        bm.global_image_start_token.to(device=device)
160	                    )
161	                    .unsqueeze(0)
162	                    .expand(bsize, -1, -1)
163	                )
164	                start_mask = torch.ones(bsize, start_tok.shape[1], dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
165	                embs.append(start_tok)
166	                pad_masks.append(start_mask)
167	                att_masks_list += [0] * start_tok.shape[1]
168	
169	            img_emb = vis_tokens[:, offset : offset + n_tokens, :]
170	            offset += n_tokens
171	
172	            img_emb_dim = img_emb.shape[-1]
173	            img_emb = img_emb * torch.tensor(
174	                img_emb_dim**0.5, dtype=img_emb.dtype, device=device
175	            )
176	
177	            cam_mask = img_mask[:, None].expand(bsize, n_tokens)
178	            embs.append(img_emb)
179	            pad_masks.append(cam_mask)
180	            att_masks_list += [0] * n_tokens
181	
182	            if bm.add_image_special_tokens:
183	                end_tok = (
184	                    bm.vlm_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(
185	                        bm.image_end_token.to(device=device)
186	                    )
187	                    .unsqueeze(0)
188	                    .expand(bsize, -1, -1)
189	                )
190	                end_mask = torch.ones(bsize, end_tok.shape[1], dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
191	                embs.append(end_tok)
192	                pad_masks.append(end_mask)
193	                att_masks_list += [0] * end_tok.shape[1]
194	
195	        lang_emb = bm.vlm_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(lang_tokens)
196	        lang_emb = lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb.shape[-1])
197	        embs.append(lang_emb)
198	        pad_masks.append(lang_masks)
199	        att_masks_list += [0] * lang_emb.shape[1]
200	
201	        state_emb = bm.state_proj(state)
202	        if state_emb.ndim == 2:
203	            state_emb = state_emb[:, None, :]
204	        states_seq = state_emb.shape[1]
205	        state_mask = torch.ones(bsize, states_seq, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
206	        embs.append(state_emb)
207	        pad_masks.append(state_mask)
208	        att_masks_list += [1] * states_seq
209	
210	        embs_cat = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)
211	        pad_cat = torch.cat(pad_masks, dim=1)
212	        att_cat = torch.tensor(att_masks_list, dtype=torch.bool, device=device).unsqueeze(0)
213	
214	        prefix_len = bm.prefix_length
215	        if pad_cat.shape[1] < prefix_len:
216	            embs_cat = _pad_tensor(embs_cat, prefix_len, pad_value=0)
217	            pad_cat = _pad_tensor(pad_cat, prefix_len, pad_value=0)
218	            att_cat = _pad_tensor(att_cat, prefix_len, pad_value=0)
219	
220	        att_cat = att_cat.expand(bsize, -1)
221	        return embs_cat, pad_cat, att_cat
222	
223	    def forward(
224	        self, batch: Dict[str, Tensor], step: int = 0
225	    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Dict[str, Any]]:
226	        """Full IMM forward: ACIS scoring → token selection → VLM+Expert → action + margin loss.
227	
228	        Args:
229	            batch: LeRobot-format batch with observation.images.*, observation.state,
230	                   observation.language.tokens, observation.language.attention_mask, action.
231	            step: current training step (for margin loss warmup).
232	        Returns:
233	            total_loss: scalar.
234	            info: dict with diagnostics (importance_scores, k, margin, lipschitz, etc.).
235	        """
236	        bm = self.base_model
237	
238	        images, img_masks = self.policy.prepare_images(batch)
239	        state = self.policy.prepare_state(batch)
240	        lang_tokens = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS]
241	        lang_masks = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK]
242	        actions = self.policy.prepare_action(batch)
243	
244	        vis_tokens, tokens_per_cam = self._get_visual_tokens(images)
245	
246	        noise = bm.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)
247	        time = bm.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)
248	        time_expanded = time[:, None, None]
249	        x_t = time_expanded * noise + (1 - time_expanded) * actions
250	        u_t = noise - actions
251	
252	        suffix_embs, suffix_pad, suffix_att = bm.embed_suffix(x_t, time)
253	
254	        importance_scores = self.acis(suffix_embs, vis_tokens)
255	
256	        N_vis = vis_tokens.shape[1]
257	        k = max(1, int(N_vis * self.k_ratio))
258	        selected_tokens, mask, sel_info = self.token_selector(
259	            importance_scores, vis_tokens, k, training=self.training
260	        )
261	
262	        prefix_embs, prefix_pad, prefix_att = self._build_prefix_with_vis_tokens(
263	            selected_tokens, tokens_per_cam, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state
264	        )
265	
266	        all_pad = torch.cat([prefix_pad, suffix_pad], dim=1)
267	        all_att = torch.cat([prefix_att, suffix_att], dim=1)
268	        att_2d = _make_att_2d_masks(all_pad, all_att)
269	        position_ids = torch.cumsum(all_pad, dim=1) - 1
270	
271	        (_, suffix_out), _ = bm.vlm_with_expert.forward(
272	            attention_mask=att_2d,
273	            position_ids=position_ids,
274	            past_key_values=None,
275	            inputs_embeds=[prefix_embs, suffix_embs],
276	            use_cache=False,
277	            fill_kv_cache=False,
278	        )
279	
280	        chunk_size = bm.config.chunk_size
281	        suffix_out = suffix_out[:, -chunk_size:]
282	        suffix_out = suffix_out.to(dtype=torch.float32)
283	        v_t = bm.action_out_proj(suffix_out)
284	
285	        action_dim = self.policy.config.action_feature.shape[0]
286	        action_loss = F.mse_loss(
287	            u_t[:, :, :action_dim],
288	            v_t[:, :, :action_dim],
289	        )
290	
291	        total_loss, imm_info = self.imm_loss(action_loss, importance_scores, k, step)
292	
293	        info = {
294	            **sel_info,
295	            **imm_info,
296	            "importance_scores": importance_scores.detach(),
297	            "k": k,
298	            "k_ratio": self.k_ratio,
299	            "lipschitz_estimate": self.acis.get_lipschitz_estimate(),
300	        }
301	        return total_loss, info
302	
303	    def forward_baseline(self, batch: Dict[str, Tensor]) -> Tuple[Tensor, Dict[str, Any]]:
304	        """Baseline forward: delegate to the original SmolVLAPolicy (no ACIS)."""
305	        loss, loss_dict = self.policy.forward(batch)
306	        return loss, {"action_loss": loss.detach().item() if loss.dim() == 0 else loss.detach()}
307	
308	    @torch.no_grad()
309	    def compute_importance_scores(
310	        self, batch: Dict[str, Tensor]
311	    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, int]:
312	        """Extract ACIS scores without running full forward (for evaluation)."""
313	        bm = self.base_model
314	
315	        images, _ = self.policy.prepare_images(batch)
316	        vis_tokens, _ = self._get_visual_tokens(images)
317	
318	        actions = self.policy.prepare_action(batch)
319	        noise = bm.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)
320	        time = bm.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)
321	        time_expanded = time[:, None, None]
322	        x_t = time_expanded * noise + (1 - time_expanded) * actions
323	
324	        suffix_embs, _, _ = bm.embed_suffix(x_t, time)
325	        scores = self.acis(suffix_embs, vis_tokens)
326	
327	        N_vis = vis_tokens.shape[1]
328	        k = max(1, int(N_vis * self.k_ratio))
329	        return scores, k
330	
331	
332	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
333	# Minimal mock for standalone testing (no lerobot dependency)
334	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
335	class _MockConfig:
336	    def __init__(self, action_dim=32, chunk_size=50, prefix_length=-1):
337	        self.chunk_size = chunk_size
338	        self.prefix_length = prefix_length
339	        self.max_action_dim = action_dim
340	        self.action_feature = type("F", (), {"shape": (action_dim,)})()
341	        self.image_features = ["observation.images.agentview", "observation.images.wrist"]
342	
343	
344	class _MockTextConfig:
345	    def __init__(self, hidden_size=960):
346	        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
347	
348	
349	class _MockVLMConfig:
350	    def __init__(self, hidden_size=960):
351	        self.text_config = _MockTextConfig(hidden_size)
352	
353	
354	class _MockVLMWithExpert(nn.Module):
355	    def __init__(self, hidden_dim=960, expert_hidden=720):
356	        super().__init__()
357	        self.embed_proj = nn.Linear(3 * 224 * 224, hidden_dim)
358	        self.lang_embed = nn.Embedding(1000, hidden_dim)
359	        self.expert_hidden_size = expert_hidden
360	        self.config = _MockVLMConfig(hidden_dim)
361	
362	    def embed_image(self, image: Tensor) -> Tensor:
363	        B = image.shape[0]
364	        D = self.config.text_config.hidden_size
365	        return torch.randn(B, 64, D, device=image.device, dtype=image.dtype)
366	
367	    def embed_language_tokens(self, tokens: Tensor) -> Tensor:
368	        if tokens.dtype in (torch.float32, torch.float16, torch.bfloat16):
369	            return tokens
370	        return self.lang_embed(tokens.clamp(0, 999))
371	
372	    def forward(self, attention_mask, position_ids, past_key_values,
373	                inputs_embeds, use_cache, fill_kv_cache):
374	        prefix, suffix = inputs_embeds
375	        B = suffix.shape[0]
376	        suffix_out = torch.randn_like(suffix)
377	        return (prefix, suffix_out), None
378	
379	
380	class _MockVLAFlowMatching(nn.Module):
381	    def __init__(self, hidden_dim=960, expert_hidden=720, action_dim=7, chunk_size=50):
382	        super().__init__()
383	        self.vlm_with_expert = _MockVLMWithExpert(hidden_dim, expert_hidden)
384	        self.state_proj = nn.Linear(32, hidden_dim)
385	        self.action_in_proj = nn.Linear(action_dim, expert_hidden)
386	        self.action_out_proj = nn.Linear(expert_hidden, action_dim)
387	        self.action_time_mlp_in = nn.Linear(expert_hidden * 2, expert_hidden)
388	        self.action_time_mlp_out = nn.Linear(expert_hidden, expert_hidden)
389	        self.add_image_special_tokens = False
390	        self.prefix_length = -1
391	        self.config = _MockConfig(action_dim, chunk_size, prefix_length=-1)
392	        self.global_image_start_token = torch.tensor([0, 1], dtype=torch.long)
393	        self.image_end_token = torch.tensor([0], dtype=torch.long)
394	
395	    def sample_noise(self, shape, device):
396	        return torch.randn(shape, device=device)
397	
398	    def sample_time(self, bsize, device):
399	        return torch.rand(bsize, device=device) * 0.998 + 0.001
400	
401	    def embed_suffix(self, noisy_actions, timestep):
402	        action_emb = self.action_in_proj(noisy_actions)
403	        B, T, D = action_emb.shape
404	        device = action_emb.device
405	        time_emb = timestep[:, None, None].expand(B, T, D)
406	        fused = self.action_time_mlp_out(F.silu(self.action_time_mlp_in(
407	            torch.cat([action_emb, time_emb], dim=-1)
408	        )))
409	        pad_masks = torch.ones(B, T, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
410	        att_masks = torch.ones(B, T, dtype=fused.dtype, device=device)
411	        return fused, pad_masks, att_masks
412	
413	
414	class _MockSmolVLAPolicy:
415	    """Mimics SmolVLAPolicy interface for standalone testing."""
416	
417	    def __init__(self, action_dim=32, state_dim=32, chunk_size=50, num_cameras=2,
418	                 hidden_dim=960, expert_hidden=720):
419	        self.model = _MockVLAFlowMatching(
420	            hidden_dim=hidden_dim, expert_hidden=expert_hidden,
421	            action_dim=action_dim, chunk_size=chunk_size,
422	        )
423	        self.config = _MockConfig(action_dim, chunk_size)
424	        self._num_cameras = num_cameras
425	        self._state_dim = state_dim
426	        self._action_dim = action_dim
427	
428	    def prepare_images(self, batch):
429	        images = []
430	        img_masks = []
431	        for key in self.config.image_features[:self._num_cameras]:
432	            if key in batch:
433	                img = batch[key]
434	            else:
435	                img = torch.randn(
436	                    list(batch.values())[0].shape[0], 3, 224, 224,
437	                    device=list(batch.values())[0].device,
438	                )
439	            B = img.shape[0]
440	            images.append(img)
441	            img_masks.append(torch.ones(B, dtype=torch.bool, device=img.device))
442	        return images, img_masks
443	
444	    def prepare_state(self, batch):
445	        if OBS_STATE in batch:
446	            return batch[OBS_STATE]
447	        B = list(batch.values())[0].shape[0]
448	        device = list(batch.values())[0].device
449	        return torch.randn(B, self._state_dim, device=device)
450	
451	    def prepare_action(self, batch):
452	        if ACTION in batch:
453	            return batch[ACTION]
454	        B = list(batch.values())[0].shape[0]
455	        device = list(batch.values())[0].device
456	        return torch.randn(B, self.config.chunk_size, self._action_dim, device=device)
457	
458	    def forward(self, batch, **kwargs):
459	        B = list(batch.values())[0].shape[0]
460	        device = list(batch.values())[0].device
461	        loss = torch.tensor(1.0, device=device, requires_grad=True)
462	        return loss, {"loss": 1.0}
463	
464	
465	if __name__ == "__main__":
466	    torch.manual_seed(42)
467	    B = 2
468	    device = "cpu"
469	
470	    HIDDEN = 960
471	    EXPERT = 720
472	    ACTION_DIM = 32
473	    mock_policy = _MockSmolVLAPolicy(
474	        action_dim=ACTION_DIM, state_dim=32, chunk_size=50, num_cameras=2,
475	        hidden_dim=HIDDEN, expert_hidden=EXPERT,
476	    )
477	    wrapper = IMMSmolVLAWrapper(
478	        smolvla_policy=mock_policy,
479	        acis_config={"num_heads": 8},
480	        imm_config={"lambda_margin": 0.1, "delta": 0.5, "warmup_steps": 100, "k_ratio": 0.5},
481	    )
482	
483	    batch = {
484	        "observation.images.agentview": torch.randn(B, 3, 224, 224),
485	        "observation.images.wrist": torch.randn(B, 3, 224, 224),
486	        ACTION: torch.randn(B, 50, ACTION_DIM),
487	        OBS_STATE: torch.randn(B, 32),
488	        OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS: torch.randint(0, 100, (B, 20)),
489	        OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK: torch.ones(B, 20, dtype=torch.bool),
490	    }
491	
492	    wrapper.train()
493	    total_loss, info = wrapper(batch, step=50)
494	    total_loss.backward()
495	    print(f"[train] total_loss={total_loss.item():.4f}, k={info['k']}, "
496	          f"lipschitz={info['lipschitz_estimate']:.4f}")
497	
498	    base_loss, base_info = wrapper.forward_baseline(batch)
499	    print(f"[baseline] action_loss={base_loss.item():.4f}")
500	
501	    wrapper.eval()
502	    scores, k = wrapper.compute_importance_scores(batch)
503	    print(f"[eval] scores shape={scores.shape}, k={k}")
504	    print("All wrapper tests passed!")

MarginAwareTokenSelector\n\ntry:\n    from lerobot.utils.constants import (\n        ACTION,\n        OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK,\n        OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS,\n        OBS_STATE,\n    )\nexcept masks.\n\n    att_masks uses cumsum convention: 0 = bidirectional block, 1 = new causal block.\n    \"\"\"\n    cumsum = torch.cumsum(att_masks, dim=1)\n    att_2d = cumsum[:, None, :] <= cumsum[:, :, \"action_dim\" not in acis_config:\n            vlm_hidden = self.base_model.vlm_with_expert.config.text_config.hidden_size\n            expert_hidden = self.acis = ACIS(**acis_config)\n\n        self.k_ratio = imm_config.pop(\"k_ratio\", 0.5)\n        selector_temp = imm_config.pop(\"selector_temperature\", 1.0)\n\n        self.imm_loss = IMMTotalLoss(**imm_config)\n        self.token_selector = MarginAwareTokenSelector(temperature=selector_temp)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _unwrap_to_vla(model: nn.Module) -> nn.Module:\n        \"\"\"Unwrap PeftModel → LoraModel → VLAFlowMatching.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from peft import PeftModel\n            if isinstance(model, PeftModel):\n                return each camera image through SigLIP+connector.\n\n        Returns:\n            vis_tokens: (B, sum(n_i), D) concatenated visual tokens from all cameras.\n            tokens_per_camera: list of token counts per camera.\n        \"\"\"\n        all_vis = []\n        tokens_per_camera = []\n        for img in images:\n            vis = self.base_model.vlm_with_expert.embed_image(img)\n            all_vis.append(vis)\n            tokens_per_camera.append(vis.shape[1])\n        return torch.cat(all_vis, dim=1), tokens_per_camera\n\n    def _build_prefix_with_vis_tokens(\n        self,\n Tensor, Tensor]:\n        \"\"\"Reconstruct prefix embeddings using pre-computed (possibly masked) visual tokens.\n\n        Replicates VLAFlowMatching.embed_prefix() but substitutes the visual token\n        computation with externally provided tokens (after ACIS masking).\n\n        Returns:\n            embs:      (B, prefix_length, D)\n            pad_masks: (B, prefix_length) bool\n bm.global_image_start_token.to(device=device)\n                    )\n                    .unsqueeze(0)\n                    .expand(bsize, -1, -1)\n                )\n                start_mask = torch.ones(bsize, start_tok.shape[1], dtype=torch.bool, device=device)\n                embs.append(start_tok)\n                pad_masks.append(start_mask)\n                att_masks_list += [0] * embs.append(img_emb)\n            pad_masks.append(cam_mask)\n            att_masks_list += [0] * n_tokens\n\n            if bm.add_image_special_tokens:\n                end_tok = (\n pad_masks.append(end_mask)\n                att_masks_list += [0] * end_tok.shape[1]\n\n        lang_emb = bm.vlm_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(lang_tokens)\n        lang_emb = lang_emb * Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Full IMM forward: ACIS scoring → token selection → VLM+Expert → action + margin loss.\n\n        Args:\n            batch: LeRobot-format batch with observation.images.*, observation.state,\n                   observation.language.tokens, observation.language.attention_mask, action.\n            step: current training step (for margin loss warmup).\n        Returns:\n     device = list(batch.values())[0].device\n        return torch.randn(B, self.config.chunk_size, self._action_dim, device=device)\n\n    def forward(self, batch, **kwargs):\n        B = Used by smolvla_aloha_sim which adds the empty\\n    # left and right wrist cameras in addition to the top camera.\\n    empty_cameras: int = 0\\n\\n    # Converts the joint and gripper values from the standard Aloha space to\\n    # the space used by the pi internal runtime which was used to train the base model.\\n    adapt_to_pi_aloha: bool = False\\n\\n    # Converts joint dimensions to relative values with respect to the current state before passing to the model.\\n    # Gripper dimensions will remain in absolute values.\\n    use_delta_joint_actions_aloha: bool = False\\n\\n    # Tokenizer\\n    tokenizer_max_length: int = 48\\n\\n    # Decoding\\n    num_steps: int = 10\\n\\n    # Attention utils\\n    use_cache: bool = True\\n\\n    # Finetuning settings\\n    freeze_vision_encoder: bool = True\\n    train_expert_only: bool = True\\n    train_state_proj: bool = True\\n\\n    # Training presets\\n    optimizer_lr: float = 1e-4\\n    optimizer_betas: tuple[float, float] = (0.9, 0.95)\\n    optimizer_eps: float = 1e-8\\n    optimizer_weight_decay: float = 1e-10\\n    optimizer_grad_clip_norm: float = 10\\n\\n    scheduler_warmup_steps: int = 1_000\\n    scheduler_decay_steps: int = 30_000\\n    scheduler_decay_lr: float = 2.5e-6\\n\\n    vlm_model_name: str = \\\"HuggingFaceTB/SmolVLM2-500M-Video-Instruct\\\"  # Select the VLM backbone.\\n    load_vlm_weights: bool = False  # Set to False in case of training the expert from scratch. True when init from pretrained SmolVLA weights\\n\\n    add_image_special_tokens: bool = False  # Whether to use special image tokens around image features.\\n\\n    attention_mode: str = \\\"cross_attn\\\"\\n\\n    prefix_length: int = -1\\n\\n    pad_language_to: str = \\\"longest\\\"  # \\\"max_length\\\"\\n\\n    num_expert_layers: int = -1  # Less or equal to 0 is the default where the action expert has the same number of layers of VLM. Otherwise the expert have less layers.\\n    num_vlm_layers: int = 16  # Number of layers used in the VLM (first num_vlm_layers layers)\\n    self_attn_every_n_layers: int = 2  # Interleave SA layers each self_attn_every_n_layers\\n    expert_width_multiplier: float = 0.75  # The action expert hidden size (wrt to the VLM)\\n\\n    min_period: float = 4e-3  # sensitivity range for the timestep used in sine-cosine positional encoding\\n    max_period: float = 4.0\\n\\n    # Real-Time Chunking (RTC) configuration\\n    rtc_config: RTCConfig | None = None\\n\\n    compile_model: bool = False  # Whether to use torch.compile for model optimization\\n    compile_mode: str = \\\"max-autotune\\\"  # Torch compile mode\\n\\n    def __post_init__(self):\\n        super().__post_init__()\\n\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Input validation (not exhaustive).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if self.n_action_steps > self.chunk_size:\\n            raise ValueError(\\n                f\\\"The chunk size is the upper bound for the number of action steps per model invocation. Compute the outer product\\n    scaling_factor = 1.0 / period * 2 * math.pi\\n    sin_input = scaling_factor[None, :] * time[:, None]\\n    pos_emb = torch.cat([torch.sin(sin_input), torch.cos(sin_input)], dim=1)\\n    return pos_emb\\n\\n\\ndef make_att_2d_masks(pad_masks, att_masks):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Copied from big_vision.\\n\\n    Tokens can attend to valid inputs tokens which have a cumulative mask_ar\\n    smaller or equal to theirs. Now I need to also check the `PreTrainedPolicy` / `PreTrainedConfig` to understand the `image_features` and `action_feature` properties.

From the code, I see:
- `self.config.image_features` - used in `prepare_images()` to iterate over image keys
- `self.config.action_feature` - used in line 295 and 380 for getting `original_action_dim`
- `self.config.max_state_dim` - used for state padding (= 32)


- `self.config.max_action_dim` - used for action padding (= 32)

The projection layers all use fixed dimensions of 32 for both state and action, regardless of their actual sizes. The state and action projections map from these padded dimensions to the hidden sizes used by the model.

So the projection layers themselves don't need modification—SmolVLA already pads everything to 32. What actually needs to change are the config properties that define the input and output feature shapes: `config.input_features` for the camera keys, `config.output_features` for the action shape used in loss trimming, and `config.action_feature` which controls the loss trimming at specific lines in the code. I should check what these config properties actually are on the PreTrainedConfig class.
```
